</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC394" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="394" style="position:relative">If you add terms to a covered work in accord with this section, you must place,</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC395" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="395" style="position:relative">in the relevant source files, a statement of the additional terms that apply to</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC396" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="396" style="position:relative">those files, or a notice indicating where to find the applicable terms.</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC397" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="397" style="position:relative">
</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC398" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="398" style="position:relative">Additional terms, permissive or non-permissive, may be stated in the form of a</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC399" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="399" style="position:relative">separately written license, or stated as exceptions; the above requirements</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC400" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="400" style="position:relative">apply either way.</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC401" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="401" style="position:relative">
</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC402" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="402" style="position:relative">8. Termination.</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC403" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="403" style="position:relative">You may not propagate or modify a covered work except as expressly provided</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC404" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="404" style="position:relative">under this License. Any attempt otherwise to propagate or modify it is void,</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC405" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="405" style="position:relative">and will automatically terminate your rights under this License (including any</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC406" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="406" style="position:relative">patent licenses granted under the third paragraph of section 11).</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC407" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="407" style="position:relative">
</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC408" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="408" style="position:relative">However, if you cease all violation of this License, then your license from a</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC409" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="409" style="position:relative">particular copyright holder is reinstated (a) provisionally, unless and until</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC410" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="410" style="position:relative">the copyright holder explicitly and finally terminates your license, and (b)</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC411" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="411" style="position:relative">permanently, if the copyright holder fails to notify you of the violation by</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC412" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="412" style="position:relative">some reasonable means prior to 60 days after the cessation.</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC413" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="413" style="position:relative">
</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC414" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="414" style="position:relative">Moreover, your license from a particular copyright holder is reinstated</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC415" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="415" style="position:relative">permanently if the copyright holder notifies you of the violation by some</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC416" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="416" style="position:relative">reasonable means, this is the first time you have received notice of violation</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC417" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="417" style="position:relative">of this License (for any work) from that copyright holder, and you cure the</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC418" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="418" style="position:relative">violation prior to 30 days after your receipt of the notice.</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC419" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="419" style="position:relative">
</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC420" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="420" style="position:relative">Termination of your rights under this section does not terminate the licenses</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC421" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="421" style="position:relative">of parties who have received copies or rights from you under this License. If</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC422" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="422" style="position:relative">your rights have been terminated and not permanently reinstated, you do not</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC423" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="423" style="position:relative">qualify to receive new licenses for the same material under section 10.</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC424" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="424" style="position:relative">
</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC425" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="425" style="position:relative">9. Acceptance Not Required for Having Copies.</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC426" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="426" style="position:relative">You are not required to accept this License in order to receive or run a copy</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC427" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="427" style="position:relative">of the Program. Ancillary propagation of a covered work occurring solely as a</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC428" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="428" style="position:relative">consequence of using peer-to-peer transmission to receive a copy likewise does</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC429" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="429" style="position:relative">not require acceptance. However, nothing other than this License grants you</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC430" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="430" style="position:relative">permission to propagate or modify any covered work. These actions infringe</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC431" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="431" style="position:relative">copyright if you do not accept this License. Therefore, by modifying or</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC432" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="432" style="position:relative">propagating a covered work, you indicate your acceptance of this License to do</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC433" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="433" style="position:relative">so.</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC434" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="434" style="position:relative">
</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC435" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="435" style="position:relative">10. Automatic Licensing of Downstream Recipients.</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC436" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="436" style="position:relative">Each time you convey a covered work, the recipient automatically receives a</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC437" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="437" style="position:relative">license from the original licensors, to run, modify and propagate that work,</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC438" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="438" style="position:relative">subject to this License. You are not responsible for enforcing compliance by</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC439" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="439" style="position:relative">third parties with this License.</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC440" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="440" style="position:relative">
</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC441" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="441" style="position:relative">An “entity transaction” is a transaction transferring control of an</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC442" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="442" style="position:relative">organization, or substantially all assets of one, or subdividing an</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC443" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="443" style="position:relative">organization, or merging organizations. If propagation of a covered work</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC444" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="444" style="position:relative">results from an entity transaction, each party to that transaction who receives</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC445" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="445" style="position:relative">a copy of the work also receives whatever licenses to the work the party&#039;s</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC446" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="446" style="position:relative">predecessor in interest had or could give under the previous paragraph, plus a</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC447" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="447" style="position:relative">right to possession of the Corresponding Source of the work from the</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC448" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="448" style="position:relative">predecessor in interest, if the predecessor has it or can get it with</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC449" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="449" style="position:relative">reasonable efforts.</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC450" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="450" style="position:relative">
</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC451" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="451" style="position:relative">You may not impose any further restrictions on the exercise of the rights</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC452" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="452" style="position:relative">granted or affirmed under this License. For example, you may not impose a</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC453" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="453" style="position:relative">license fee, royalty, or other charge for exercise of rights granted under this</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC454" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="454" style="position:relative">License, and you may not initiate litigation (including a cross-claim or</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC455" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="455" style="position:relative">counterclaim in a lawsuit) alleging that any patent claim is infringed by</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC456" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="456" style="position:relative">making, using, selling, offering for sale, or importing the Program or any</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC457" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="457" style="position:relative">portion of it.</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC458" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="458" style="position:relative">
</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC459" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="459" style="position:relative">11. Patents.</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC460" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="460" style="position:relative">A “contributor” is a copyright holder who authorizes use under this License of</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC461" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="461" style="position:relative">the Program or a work on which the Program is based. The work thus licensed is</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC462" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="462" style="position:relative">called the contributor&#039;s “contributor version”.</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC463" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="463" style="position:relative">
</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC464" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="464" style="position:relative">A contributor&#039;s “essential patent claims” are all patent claims owned or</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC465" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="465" style="position:relative">controlled by the contributor, whether already acquired or hereafter acquired,</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC466" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="466" style="position:relative">that would be infringed by some manner, permitted by this License, of making,</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC467" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="467" style="position:relative">using, or selling its contributor version, but do not include claims that would</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC468" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="468" style="position:relative">be infringed only as a consequence of further modification of the contributor</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC469" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="469" style="position:relative">version. For purposes of this definition, “control” includes the right to grant</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC470" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="470" style="position:relative">patent sublicenses in a manner consistent with the requirements of this</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC471" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="471" style="position:relative">License.</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC472" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="472" style="position:relative">
</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC473" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="473" style="position:relative">Each contributor grants you a non-exclusive, worldwide, royalty-free patent</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC474" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="474" style="position:relative">license under the contributor&#039;s essential patent claims, to make, use, sell,</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC475" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="475" style="position:relative">offer for sale, import and otherwise run, modify and propagate the contents of</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC476" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="476" style="position:relative">its contributor version.</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC477" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="477" style="position:relative">
</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC478" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="478" style="position:relative">In the following three paragraphs, a “patent license” is any express agreement</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC479" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="479" style="position:relative">or commitment, however denominated, not to enforce a patent (such as an express</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC480" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="480" style="position:relative">permission to practice a patent or covenant not to sue for patent</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC481" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="481" style="position:relative">infringement). To “grant” such a patent license to a party means to make such</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC482" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="482" style="position:relative">an agreement or commitment not to enforce a patent against the party.</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC483" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="483" style="position:relative">
</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC484" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="484" style="position:relative">If you convey a covered work, knowingly relying on a patent license, and the</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC485" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="485" style="position:relative">Corresponding Source of the work is not available for anyone to copy, free of</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC486" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="486" style="position:relative">charge and under the terms of this License, through a publicly available</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC487" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell" data-line-number="487" style="position:relative">network server or other readily accessible means, then you must either (1)</div></div></div><div class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents" style="min-height:auto"><div><div id="LC488" class="react-file-line html-div" data-testid="code-cell"
